# 2. تفاصيل الملفات والأكواد البرمجية والصور (Files Content & Assets Map)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
📌 اسم الملف     : Game Icons Studio.bat
📂 المسار النسبي : Game Icons Studio.bat
🔗 المسار الكامل : C:\Users\yahya\Downloads\Game-Icons-Studio-v6.27\Game-Icons-Studio-v6.27\Game Icons Studio.bat
📊 الحجم         : 1.36 KB
🏷️ النوع         : [ملف كود / إعدادات] (.bat)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
```bat
@echo off
title Game Icons Studio
setlocal
cd /d "%~dp0"

rem ============================================================
rem  Game Icons Studio - Launcher
rem  From generic icons to a game shelf.
rem  Everything lives under data\ ; only this launcher and the
rem  shortcut sit in the main folder.
rem ============================================================

rem One-time: create the custom-icon shortcut in the main folder
if not exist "%~dp0Game Icons Studio.lnk" (
    where pwsh >nul 2>&1
    if %errorlevel%==0 (
        pwsh -NoLogo -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0data\Create-Shortcut.ps1" >nul 2>&1
    ) else (
        powershell -NoLogo -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0data\Create-Shortcut.ps1" >nul 2>&1
    )
)

where pwsh >nul 2>&1
if %errorlevel%==0 (
    pwsh -NoLogo -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0data\src\Main.ps1"
) else (
    echo [INFO] PowerShell 7 not found. Falling back to Windows PowerShell 5.1.
    echo [INFO] Parallel downloads will be disabled. Install PowerShell 7 for best performance.
    echo.
    powershell -NoLogo -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0data\src\Main.ps1"
)

rem Close automatically on a clean exit; pause only if something failed.
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo Something went wrong. Press a key to close.
    pause >nul
)
endlocal
exit
```

```md
v6.27 - Removed the backup/restore feature entirely. The whole backup system is gone: BackupTools module deleted; backup creation removed from the apply pipeline; the backup-list, restore-backup and undo-last API routes removed; the Restore original icons home card, the backup manager screen, the Undo last apply button, and all related code removed. All mentions of backups/restore/undo were removed from the UI and the What is GIS tutorial so nothing gives misleading information. The home screen now offers Repair icon cache in place of Restore (it clears the Windows icon cache and restarts Explorer, which now makes that feature reliable). Backup may be reconsidered in a future version. NOTE: applying icons is no longer reversible from within the app.

v6.26 - Fixed the backup/restore feature. The backup itself was always being created correctly and the original shortcuts were being copied back on restore; the problem was that Windows kept showing the cached (new) icons, so it looked like restore did nothing. Restore (both Undo last apply and the backup manager) now forces a real icon refresh: it clears the Windows icon cache and restarts the Explorer shell so your original icons reappear immediately (the taskbar briefly flashes, which is normal). The general icon refresh after apply is also stronger now (adds ie4uinit).

v6.25 - The What is GIS? tour is now easy to reach. A permanent What is GIS button sits in the top bar, right next to Home (question-mark icon). The first-run API key screen has a big What is GIS? button at the top, so new users can take the tour before setting up. The tour now ends with a Go to home to start button (which returns to setup if no API key is set yet, otherwise home).

v6.24 - Simplification + a tutorial. The summary screen no longer has clickable numbers; instead it has two clear choices: Finish & open my folder, or Return to the grid to review and change any icons (multi-select then change, just like the review step). The Export choices / Import choices feature has been completely removed (buttons, routes, backend, and the unused file dialogs). New What is GIS? page (reachable from the home tools row) explains, at a glance, what the app is, that it uses the free SteamGridDB API, how it works in 3 steps, what each button does, the summary screen, and the safe-by-design backups.

v6.23 - Optimization pass. Removed redundant disk writes on the scan path: the settings file is now only rewritten when the remembered folder actually changes, and the known-games file is only rewritten when that folders game list actually changes (previously both were written on every scan). Full audit confirmed: no new dead code from v6.22, clean 1:1 client/server API contract, all resources disposed, JSON already compressed. The legacy console-menu functions are intentionally retained as a safe fallback.

v6.22 - Four new tools on the home screen. Export choices / Import choices: save all your remembered covers, game matches and known-game lists to a single file, then load them into any future version so you never have to redo your picks. Quick re-apply: one click re-applies your last saved choices to your last folder end to end (handy after an icon-cache glitch). History: a searchable log of what icons you changed, for which game, and when. Plus the scan and apply screens now show a rough time estimate.

v6.21 - Performance and responsiveness pass. Added a per-session cover cache so re-opening a games covers (wrong-game / re-pick / report edits) is instant with no repeat API calls. Smarter image preloading: during scanning only the single best cover per game is warmed (freeing bandwidth for the scan), and the full cover set for a game is preloaded the moment you arrive at it. Long review/summary grids now use content-visibility so off-screen tiles are skipped while scrolling, keeping big libraries smooth. Trimmed artificial delays in the apply screen (livelier phase messages, faster hand-off to the summary). No core logic changed.

v6.20 - Auto-scan: selecting a folder (Browse or paste+Enter) now starts the scan immediately, no separate Scan click. Plus a full code audit and optimization pass: removed dead code (unused /api/backup-status route, Get-BackupSummary, an unused Set-Fg helper, the obsolete editFromReport path and its state flag), confirmed a clean 1:1 client/server API contract (25/25), verified all 108 functions resolve with no broken references, and removed a redundant second folder scan on the scan-begin path. All modules pass structural validation (balanced braces, ASCII-clean) and the client script passes a syntax check.

v6.19 - Removed the single-game quick mode (the type-a-game-name box) and its backend filter, per request. The folder screen is back to a clean Browse / paste / Scan. Cover Grid/Gallery toggle and new-game detection remain.

v6.18 - Made the Phase C features visible. After you browse or paste a folder, the app now stays on the folder screen (instead of auto-scanning) so you can clearly see all three options: Scan this folder, the single-game quick mode box (fix just one game), and the new-games offer when the folder was scanned before. The cover Grid/Gallery toggle is on the Choose a different cover screen.

v6.17 (Phase C) - Three new features. Single-game quick mode: on the folder screen, type one game name and fix just that shortcut without scanning everything. Cover gallery mode: the cover picker now has a Grid/Gallery toggle, with a large arrow-key navigable gallery view (remembered between runs). New-game detection: Game Icons Studio remembers the games in each folder and, on your next visit, offers to set icons for just the newly added games or scan everything.

v6.16 - Reverted the language switch (English only for now) and removed the drag-and-drop folder feature, which could not get the real folder path from the browser. The app is back to a clean, stable English-only base. The summary stat pages (Updated/Matched/Placeholder) still behave like the Change Icons screen with multi-select, and the top-bar zoom buttons still show clean + and - symbols.

v6.15 (Phase B, part 1) - Language switch: the app is English by default with a flag button (top bar) to switch to Turkish, just like the theme switcher; your choice is remembered. Drag-and-drop: you can drag a game folder onto the folder screen. The summary stat pages (Updated/Matched/Placeholder) now behave exactly like the Change Icons screen: multi-select the ones you want, then Change these icons; the change button becomes primary when items are selected. Zoom buttons in the top bar now show clean + and - symbols only.

v6.14 (Phase A) - Restore now works fully in the browser: a backup manager lists every dated backup (with game names) and restores any one, plus a one-click Repair icon cache for blank/stale icons. New pre-scan folder health check (standard vs launcher vs broken shortcuts). New final confirmation before applying, which also warns when two shortcuts map to the same game (duplicate detection). Internet Shortcut conversion already shows a success message. (Phase B next: language switch, cover gallery, before/after, single-game quick mode, drag-and-drop.)

v6.13 - Memory: Game Icons Studio now remembers the games and covers you picked, and on the next scan asks whether to reuse your previous choices or start fresh. Picking reuse auto-applies remembered covers and skips re-asking. Internet Shortcut conversion now shows a clear "Conversion successful!" confirmation. Review screen has a live search box to filter your games by name. The summary now includes a "This run at a glance" stats panel (processed, updated, success rate, placeholders) and an "Undo last apply" button that restores your most recent backup in one click.

v6.12 - The brand icon is now fully transparent and is used everywhere (header, favicon, app/window icon, launcher) as the permanent visual identity. The black-background header image was dropped. The setup screen now has two clear buttons: Create a SteamGridDB account and Get my API key, each with its own short guide. Review screen retitled and now always opens at the top; when you select icons, Finish is hidden and Change these icons becomes the main button. Fullscreen toggle reads Exit Fullscreen. New: Game Icons Studio detects launcher Internet Shortcuts (.url) and offers to convert them to standard .lnk shortcuts so their icons can be customized, keeping them fully working.

v6.11 - Added official branding (header logo, brand symbol, app icon) throughout the app. The setup screen now has a friendly mini tutorial explaining how to get a free SteamGridDB API key (create an account or log in with Steam). Buttons are now modern colored gradients with a hover glow: green for confirm/finish, yellow for change-cover/change-icons, red for wrong-game/start-over. The match question is now the friendlier "Do you like this icon?" and Finish reads "FINISH! (Apply all changes)".

v6.10 - Review screen redesign: removed the checkbox overlay so covers are fully visible. Click anywhere on a cover to select it for changing (it dims with a "selected" badge); click again to deselect. The grid now fills the page and flows naturally (scroll with the mouse wheel) instead of a small inner frame. Button renamed to "Change these icons" with a live count.

v6.9 - Cleaner folder layout: the main folder now holds only the launcher (and its shortcut); everything else lives under data/. The Review screen now lets you tick multiple icons and use "Change selected", or click any single cover to change it; after each change you return to the Review grid. A "Return to grid" button is always available while changing an icon.

v6.8 - Glass is now the default theme. Your theme choice (and text size) is remembered between launches.

v6.7 - Added two more themes: Modern Dark (teal, soft, rounded) and Modern Light (warm cream, terracotta). The theme switcher now cycles through seven looks.

v6.6 - Tidied the project: all code now lives in a src/ folder, keeping the root clean (just the launcher, README, Assets and your data). Added a one-click theme switcher with five looks: Dark, Light, Glass (frosted), Gaming (neon), and Minimal.

v6.5 - Removed the green tick animation for a calmer flow. Friendlier scan wording ("Scanning SteamGridDB for your games"). Theme switch now shows a sun/moon icon. Clear "no cover art" screen with a Generate-a-placeholder button (automatic in auto mode). The summary numbers (Updated/Matched/Placeholder/Skipped/Failed) are now clickable: each opens a grid of those games, and from Matched/Placeholder you can click a game to change its icon. Review screen is now a grid of covers you click to change. Picking a cover auto-advances to the next game. "Open my folder & finish" opens your games folder in Large Icons view and closes the app. Home button renamed to "Choose your game shortcuts folder".

v6.4 - Fixed the freeze where the folder/file picker blocked everything and windows opened all at once: native pickers now run on their own thread so the app stays responsive. The console window minimizes itself once the browser opens, so it never steals focus again. Keyboard shortcuts no longer touch Ctrl/Cmd/Alt combos or Tab, so copy/paste and normal Windows shortcuts work everywhere. Auto mode is much more hands-off: it accepts the top match unless the score is genuinely low, and shows a clear banner while it works. Layout is responsive now and no longer clips text in a windowed (non-fullscreen) view.

v6.3 - API key is now entered in the browser (Get key button + Paste button). Folder picker and file picker pop to the front and the folder choice auto-continues (no second click, no hunting for the console window). Scanning is much faster: only the best match plus its covers are fetched up front, and alternatives load on demand when you say a game is wrong. Covers preload so the wizard feels instant. Keyboard + and - resize the UI. Every screen has Back and Home. The apply phase uses calm fade in/out messages you can actually read. Friendlier wording throughout.

v6.2 - Browser-first by default (launches straight into the fullscreen app). New home screen with three big actions. Folder picker now pops to the foreground, plus a paste-a-path box. Full keyboard navigation (arrows move, Enter selects, Backspace goes back, F fullscreen). Working A-/A+ text scaling. Live "Matching your game shortcuts with SteamGridDB" progress with a fade-in green tick per match. Cover art is preloaded so the wizard feels instant. Auto-all shows a banner and only stops on uncertain matches. Apply phase uses a calm typing animation you can actually read.

# Game Icons Studio

**From generic icons to a game shelf.**
Download covers. Replace icons. Modernize your Games folder.

---

## Quick start

1. Double-click **Game Icons Studio** (the shortcut) or **Game Icons Studio.bat**.
2. On first launch a desktop-style shortcut with a custom icon is created automatically.
3. Choose **[1] Studio (Browser)** for the guided, fullscreen browser experience.

The browser app walks you through it: pick your games folder, confirm each match, choose a cover, review, and apply. No command-line typing.

## What it does

Game Icons Studio scans your game shortcuts (.lnk), matches each one to a game on SteamGridDB, downloads square 1:1 **Grid** cover art, builds clean multi-size .ico files, backs up your originals, and applies the new icons. Your Games folder turns into a visual shelf.

## Modes

- **[1] Studio (Browser)** - the full guided experience in your browser (recommended).
- **[2] Auto (CMD)** - scan the whole folder and apply the best match + best cover, no questions.
- **[3] Change Icons (CMD)** - pick specific shortcuts and re-choose game + cover.
- **[4] Restore from backup** - put back any previous icons.
- **[5] Cache management** - clear caches, or CLEAN EVERYTHING for a fresh start.

## Requirements

- Windows 10/11
- PowerShell 7+ recommended (falls back to Windows PowerShell 5.1)
- A free SteamGridDB API key (entered once): https://www.steamgriddb.com/profile/preferences/api

## Notes

- Grid (square) covers only. Icon and Logo modes were removed to keep the flow simple.
- The browser mode runs a small local server on your machine (localhost only). Nothing is uploaded anywhere except your own requests to SteamGridDB.
- Your original icons are always backed up before any change. Use Restore to undo.

## Version

v6.1 - Live scan progress (shows the game being matched in real time), a one-click "Choose all games & covers automatically" button that only stops for uncertain matches, A-/A+ text scaling, a Fullscreen button, and a labelled progress bar during downloading/building/applying.
v6.0 - Rebranded to Game Icons Studio, Grid-only, new browser-first guided wizard, light/dark themes, keyboard navigation.
```

📌 اسم الملف     : Create-Shortcut.ps1
📂 المسار النسبي : data\Create-Shortcut.ps1
🔗 المسار الكامل : C:\Users\yahya\Downloads\Game-Icons-Studio-v6.27\Game-Icons-Studio-v6.27\data\Create-Shortcut.ps1
📊 الحجم         : 1.88 KB
🏷️ النوع         : [ملف كود / إعدادات] (.ps1)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
```ps1
# ============================================================
#  Create-Shortcut.ps1 - Game Icons Studio
#  Creates a custom-icon "Game Icons Studio.lnk" in the app folder
#  (and optionally on the Desktop) that launches the .bat. This is
#  the safe, exe-like launcher: double-clickable, custom icon, no
#  antivirus false positives, no SmartScreen warnings.
#
#  Run once:  pwsh -File "Create-Shortcut.ps1"
# ============================================================

param(
    [switch]$Desktop
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
# This script lives in the data\ folder. The shortcut, however, must
# point at the launcher in the MAIN folder (data's parent) and use the
# icon under data\Assets.
$dataDir = Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path
$root    = Split-Path -Parent $dataDir          # main app folder
$bat     = Join-Path $root 'Game Icons Studio.bat'
$ico     = Join-Path $dataDir 'Assets\app.ico'

if (-not (Test-Path $bat)) {
    Write-Host "Launcher not found: $bat" -ForegroundColor Red
    return
}

function New-AppShortcut {
    param([string]$LinkPath)
    $wsh = New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell
    $sc = $wsh.CreateShortcut($LinkPath)
    $sc.TargetPath       = $bat
    $sc.WorkingDirectory = $root
    $sc.Description       = 'Game Icons Studio - From generic icons to a game shelf.'
    $sc.WindowStyle       = 1
    if (Test-Path $ico) { $sc.IconLocation = "$ico,0" }
    $sc.Save()
    Write-Host "Created: $LinkPath" -ForegroundColor Green
}

# Always (re)create the in-folder shortcut
New-AppShortcut -LinkPath (Join-Path $root 'Game Icons Studio.lnk')

# Optionally also on the Desktop
if ($Desktop) {
    $desk = [Environment]::GetFolderPath('Desktop')
    New-AppShortcut -LinkPath (Join-Path $desk 'Game Icons Studio.lnk')
}

Write-Host ''
Write-Host 'Done. Double-click "Game Icons Studio" to launch.' -ForegroundColor Cyan
```

🔗 المسار الكامل : C:\Users\yahya\Downloads\Game-Icons-Studio-v6.27\Game-Icons-Studio-v6.27\data\src\ExplorerRefresh.ps1
📊 الحجم         : 5.31 KB
🏷️ النوع         : [ملف كود / إعدادات] (.ps1)

```ps1
# ============================================================
#  ExplorerRefresh.ps1 - Tells Windows the icons changed
#  Uses the proper Shell API: SHChangeNotify(SHCNE_ASSOCCHANGED)
#  which refreshes desktop, Explorer windows and Start Menu icons
#  without killing explorer.exe.
# ============================================================

function Initialize-ShellApi {
    if (-not ('Win32.NativeShell' -as [type])) {
        Add-Type -Namespace Win32 -Name NativeShell -MemberDefinition @'
[DllImport("shell32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
public static extern void SHChangeNotify(int wEventId, uint uFlags, IntPtr dwItem1, IntPtr dwItem2);
'@
    }
}

function Update-ExplorerIcons {
    <#
        SHCNE_ASSOCCHANGED (0x08000000) + SHCNF_IDLIST (0x0000)
        forces the shell to rebuild its icon associations, which
        refreshes desktop, open Explorer windows and pinned/Start
        Menu shortcut icons. Also asks Windows' built-in icon helper
        (ie4uinit) to rebuild, which is more reliable for shortcut
        icons than SHChangeNotify alone.
    #>
    try {
        Initialize-ShellApi
        $SHCNE_ASSOCCHANGED = 0x08000000
        $SHCNF_IDLIST       = [uint32]0x0000
        [Win32.NativeShell]::SHChangeNotify($SHCNE_ASSOCCHANGED, $SHCNF_IDLIST, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero)
        # ie4uinit rebuilds the per-user icon cache without killing Explorer.
        try { Start-Process -FilePath 'ie4uinit.exe' -ArgumentList '-show' -WindowStyle Hidden -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue } catch { }
        Write-Log 'Explorer icon refresh broadcast sent (SHChangeNotify + ie4uinit).' 'OK'
    } catch {
        Write-Log "Explorer refresh failed: $($_.Exception.Message). Icons will refresh after the next sign-in." 'WARN'
    }
}

function Restart-Explorer {
    <#
        The only fully reliable way to force Windows to drop cached
        shortcut icons is to restart the Explorer shell process.
        Windows automatically relaunches Explorer, so the taskbar
        briefly disappears and returns. Used to force a full icon
        refresh when clearing the icon cache.
        Returns $true if a restart was attempted.
    #>
    try {
        # Clear the (now unlocked once Explorer stops) icon cache files,
        # then stop Explorer; Windows relaunches it automatically.
        $localApp = [Environment]::GetFolderPath('LocalApplicationData')
        Stop-Process -Name 'explorer' -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400
        $explorerCache = Join-Path $localApp 'Microsoft\Windows\Explorer'
        if (Test-Path $explorerCache) {
            foreach ($f in @(Get-ChildItem -Path $explorerCache -Filter 'iconcache*.db' -File -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
                try { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue } catch { }
            }
        }
        $legacy = Join-Path $localApp 'IconCache.db'
        if (Test-Path $legacy) { try { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $legacy -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue } catch { } }
        # Make sure Explorer is running again (it usually auto-restarts).
        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300
        if (-not (Get-Process -Name 'explorer' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
            Start-Process 'explorer.exe' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        }
        Write-Log 'Explorer restarted to force icon refresh.' 'OK'
        return $true
    } catch {
        Write-Log "Explorer restart failed: $($_.Exception.Message)" 'WARN'
        try { Start-Process 'explorer.exe' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue } catch { }
        return $false
    }
}

function Repair-IconCache {
    <#
        Rebuilds the Windows icon cache. Useful when icons appear
        blank or stale after changes. Clears the IconCache database
        files and notifies the shell. Explorer is gently restarted
        only if needed. Returns $true on success.

        SAFE: only deletes icon-cache DB files (regenerated by Windows
        automatically); never touches user data.
    #>
    try {
        $localApp = [Environment]::GetFolderPath('LocalApplicationData')
        $explorerCache = Join-Path $localApp 'Microsoft\Windows\Explorer'

        # Notify shell of an icon settings change first (often enough).
        try { Update-ExplorerIcons } catch { }

        $deleted = 0
        if (Test-Path $explorerCache) {
            foreach ($f in @(Get-ChildItem -Path $explorerCache -Filter 'iconcache*.db' -File -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
                try { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Force -ErrorAction Stop; $deleted++ } catch { }
            }
        }
        # Legacy IconCache.db in LocalAppData root
        $legacy = Join-Path $localApp 'IconCache.db'
        if (Test-Path $legacy) { try { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $legacy -Force -ErrorAction Stop; $deleted++ } catch { } }

        Write-Log "Icon cache repair: cleared $deleted cache file(s)." 'OK'
        # The cache files are usually locked while Explorer is running,
        # so restarting Explorer is the reliable way to force a refresh.
        try { Restart-Explorer } catch { try { Update-ExplorerIcons } catch { } }
        return $true
    } catch {
        Write-Log "Icon cache repair failed: $($_.Exception.Message)" 'WARN'
        return $false
    }
}
```

🔗 المسار الكامل : C:\Users\yahya\Downloads\Game-Icons-Studio-v6.27\Game-Icons-Studio-v6.27\data\src\IconTools.ps1
📊 الحجم         : 14.91 KB
🏷️ النوع         : [ملف كود / إعدادات] (.ps1)

```ps1
# ============================================================
#  IconTools.ps1 - Multi-size .ico generation (16..256 px)
#  Styling: rounded corners, border, soft shadow (all optional)
#  Placeholder cover generator for games with no artwork.
#  Uses GDI+ (System.Drawing); writes PNG-compressed ICO
#  containers by hand, preserving full alpha transparency.
# ============================================================

Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing -ErrorAction Stop

$script:IcoSizes = @(16, 32, 48, 64, 128, 256)

function Test-IsIcoFile {
    # Checks the magic bytes of a downloaded file (00 00 01 00 = ICO)
    param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Path)
    try {
        $bytes = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($Path)
        return ($bytes.Length -gt 4 -and $bytes[0] -eq 0 -and $bytes[1] -eq 0 -and $bytes[2] -eq 1 -and $bytes[3] -eq 0)
    } catch {
        return $false
    }
}

function New-RoundedRectPath {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][double]$X,
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][double]$Y,
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][double]$Width,
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][double]$Height,
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][double]$Radius
    )
    $path = New-Object System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath
    $d = [float]($Radius * 2)
    if ($d -le 0) {
        $path.AddRectangle((New-Object System.Drawing.RectangleF([float]$X, [float]$Y, [float]$Width, [float]$Height)))
        return $path
    }
    if ($d -gt $Width)  { $d = [float]$Width }
    if ($d -gt $Height) { $d = [float]$Height }
    $x = [float]$X; $y = [float]$Y; $w = [float]$Width; $h = [float]$Height
    $path.AddArc($x, $y, $d, $d, 180, 90)
    $path.AddArc($x + $w - $d, $y, $d, $d, 270, 90)
    $path.AddArc($x + $w - $d, $y + $h - $d, $d, $d, 0, 90)
    $path.AddArc($x, $y + $h - $d, $d, $d, 90, 90)
    $path.CloseFigure()
    return $path
}

function ConvertTo-GdiColor {
    # "#RRGGBB" -> System.Drawing.Color (falls back to white)
    param([string]$Hex)
    try {
        $h = $Hex.Trim().TrimStart('#')
        if ($h.Length -eq 6) {
            $r = [Convert]::ToInt32($h.Substring(0, 2), 16)
            $g = [Convert]::ToInt32($h.Substring(2, 2), 16)
            $b = [Convert]::ToInt32($h.Substring(4, 2), 16)
            return [System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(255, $r, $g, $b)
        }
    } catch { }
    return [System.Drawing.Color]::White
}

function New-StyledBitmap {
    <#
        Renders one icon frame:
        - center-crops the source to a square
        - optional soft drop shadow (sizes >= 48 px only)
        - optional rounded-corner clipping
        - optional border stroke
        Returns a 32bpp ARGB bitmap (caller must Dispose it).
    #>
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][System.Drawing.Image]$Source,
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][int]$Size,
        [int]$CornerRadiusPercent = 0,
        [int]$BorderWidthPercent = 0,
        [string]$BorderColor = '#FFFFFF',
        [bool]$ShadowEnabled = $false,
        [bool]$Contain = $false
    )

    $side = [Math]::Min($Source.Width, $Source.Height)
    $srcX = [int](($Source.Width  - $side) / 2)
    $srcY = [int](($Source.Height - $side) / 2)

    # Shadow needs breathing room around the cover
    $useShadow = ($ShadowEnabled -and $Size -ge 48)
    $margin = if ($useShadow) { [Math]::Max(2, [int]($Size * 0.06)) } else { 0 }
    $content = $Size - (2 * $margin)
    $radius = [Math]::Min([int]($content * $CornerRadiusPercent / 100.0), [int]($content / 2))

    $bmp = New-Object System.Drawing.Bitmap($Size, $Size, [System.Drawing.Imaging.PixelFormat]::Format32bppArgb)
    $g = [System.Drawing.Graphics]::FromImage($bmp)
    try {
        $g.InterpolationMode  = [System.Drawing.Drawing2D.InterpolationMode]::HighQualityBicubic
        $g.SmoothingMode      = [System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode]::AntiAlias
        $g.PixelOffsetMode    = [System.Drawing.Drawing2D.PixelOffsetMode]::HighQuality
        $g.CompositingQuality = [System.Drawing.Drawing2D.CompositingQuality]::HighQuality
        $g.Clear([System.Drawing.Color]::Transparent)

        # ---- Soft shadow: layered translucent rounded rects ----
        if ($useShadow) {
            $layers = 4
            for ($i = $layers; $i -ge 1; $i--) {
                $grow = $i
                $alpha = [int](14 + (4 * ($layers - $i)))
                $sPath = New-RoundedRectPath -X ($margin - $grow + 1) -Y ($margin - $grow + 2) `
                    -Width ($content + 2 * $grow) -Height ($content + 2 * $grow) -Radius ($radius + $grow)
                $sBrush = New-Object System.Drawing.SolidBrush([System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb($alpha, 0, 0, 0))
                $g.FillPath($sBrush, $sPath)
                $sBrush.Dispose(); $sPath.Dispose()
            }
        }

        # ---- Cover, clipped to rounded rect ----
        $clipPath = New-RoundedRectPath -X $margin -Y $margin -Width $content -Height $content -Radius $radius
        $state = $g.Save()
        $g.SetClip($clipPath)
        if ($Contain) {
            # Fit the whole image inside the square (used for LOGO
            # artwork: wide transparent logos must never be cropped).
            $scale = [Math]::Min($content / [double]$Source.Width, $content / [double]$Source.Height)
            $dw = [Math]::Max(1, [int]($Source.Width * $scale))
            $dh = [Math]::Max(1, [int]($Source.Height * $scale))
            $dx = $margin + [int](($content - $dw) / 2)
            $dy = $margin + [int](($content - $dh) / 2)
            $destRect = New-Object System.Drawing.Rectangle($dx, $dy, $dw, $dh)
            $srcRect  = New-Object System.Drawing.Rectangle(0, 0, $Source.Width, $Source.Height)
        } else {
            $destRect = New-Object System.Drawing.Rectangle($margin, $margin, $content, $content)
            $srcRect  = New-Object System.Drawing.Rectangle($srcX, $srcY, $side, $side)
        }
        $g.DrawImage($Source, $destRect, $srcRect, [System.Drawing.GraphicsUnit]::Pixel)
        $g.Restore($state)

        # ---- Border stroke ----
        if ($BorderWidthPercent -gt 0) {
            $penWidth = [Math]::Max(1.0, $content * $BorderWidthPercent / 100.0)
            $pen = New-Object System.Drawing.Pen((ConvertTo-GdiColor $BorderColor), [float]$penWidth)
            $pen.Alignment = [System.Drawing.Drawing2D.PenAlignment]::Inset
            $g.DrawPath($pen, $clipPath)
            $pen.Dispose()
        }

        $clipPath.Dispose()
    } finally {
        $g.Dispose()
    }
    return $bmp
}

function ConvertTo-Ico {
    <#
        Converts an image into a multi-size ICO (16..256 px) applying
        the configured style. Transparency preserved via PNG entries.
        A source that is already a valid .ico is copied through as-is
        (styling cannot be applied to prebuilt icons).
    #>
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$SourcePath,
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$OutputPath,
        [int]$CornerRadiusPercent = 0,
        [int]$BorderWidthPercent = 0,
        [string]$BorderColor = '#FFFFFF',
        [bool]$ShadowEnabled = $false,
        [bool]$Contain = $false
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $SourcePath)) { throw "Source image not found: $SourcePath" }

    if (Test-IsIcoFile -Path $SourcePath) {
        Copy-Item -Path $SourcePath -Destination $OutputPath -Force
        return $OutputPath
    }

    $source = $null
    $pngBlobs = @()

    try {
        $source = [System.Drawing.Image]::FromFile($SourcePath)

        foreach ($size in $script:IcoSizes) {
            $bmp = New-StyledBitmap -Source $source -Size $size `
                -CornerRadiusPercent $CornerRadiusPercent `
                -BorderWidthPercent $BorderWidthPercent `
                -BorderColor $BorderColor `
                -ShadowEnabled $ShadowEnabled `
                -Contain $Contain
            try {
                $ms = New-Object System.IO.MemoryStream
                $bmp.Save($ms, [System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png)
                $pngBlobs += ,@{ Size = $size; Data = $ms.ToArray() }
                $ms.Dispose()
            } finally {
                $bmp.Dispose()
            }
        }
    } catch {
        throw "Image conversion failed for '$SourcePath': $($_.Exception.Message)"
    } finally {
        if ($source) { $source.Dispose() }
    }

    # ---- Write the ICO container manually ----
    $fs = $null
    $writer = $null
    try {
        $fs = [System.IO.File]::Open($OutputPath, [System.IO.FileMode]::Create, [System.IO.FileAccess]::Write)
        $writer = New-Object System.IO.BinaryWriter($fs)

        $count = $pngBlobs.Count
        $writer.Write([UInt16]0)
        $writer.Write([UInt16]1)
        $writer.Write([UInt16]$count)

        $offset = 6 + (16 * $count)
        foreach ($blob in $pngBlobs) {
            $dim = if ($blob.Size -ge 256) { [byte]0 } else { [byte]$blob.Size }
            $writer.Write([byte]$dim)
            $writer.Write([byte]$dim)
            $writer.Write([byte]0)
            $writer.Write([byte]0)
            $writer.Write([UInt16]1)
            $writer.Write([UInt16]32)
            $writer.Write([UInt32]$blob.Data.Length)
            $writer.Write([UInt32]$offset)
            $offset += $blob.Data.Length
        }
        foreach ($blob in $pngBlobs) {
            $writer.Write($blob.Data)
        }
    } catch {
        throw "Failed to write ICO file '$OutputPath': $($_.Exception.Message)"
    } finally {
        if ($writer) { $writer.Dispose() }
        if ($fs) { $fs.Dispose() }
    }

    return $OutputPath
}

# ---------------- Placeholder cover generator ----------------

function Convert-HslToRgbColor {
    param([double]$H, [double]$S, [double]$L)

    $c = (1 - [Math]::Abs(2 * $L - 1)) * $S
    $hp = $H / 60.0
    $x = $c * (1 - [Math]::Abs(($hp % 2) - 1))
    $r1 = 0.0; $g1 = 0.0; $b1 = 0.0

    if     ($hp -ge 0 -and $hp -lt 1) { $r1 = $c; $g1 = $x; $b1 = 0 }
    elseif ($hp -ge 1 -and $hp -lt 2) { $r1 = $x; $g1 = $c; $b1 = 0 }
    elseif ($hp -ge 2 -and $hp -lt 3) { $r1 = 0;  $g1 = $c; $b1 = $x }
    elseif ($hp -ge 3 -and $hp -lt 4) { $r1 = 0;  $g1 = $x; $b1 = $c }
    elseif ($hp -ge 4 -and $hp -lt 5) { $r1 = $x; $g1 = 0;  $b1 = $c }
    else                              { $r1 = $c; $g1 = 0;  $b1 = $x }

    $m = $L - ($c / 2)
    $r = [int][Math]::Round(($r1 + $m) * 255)
    $g = [int][Math]::Round(($g1 + $m) * 255)
    $b = [int][Math]::Round(($b1 + $m) * 255)
    return [System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(255,
        [Math]::Max(0, [Math]::Min(255, $r)),
        [Math]::Max(0, [Math]::Min(255, $g)),
        [Math]::Max(0, [Math]::Min(255, $b)))
}

function New-PlaceholderCover {
    <#
        Generates a 512x512 PNG cover from a game name:
        deterministic hue from the name hash, dark diagonal gradient,
        accent bar, and the game title centered in bold white text.
        Returns the output path.
    #>
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$GameName,
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$OutputPath,
        [int]$Size = 512
    )

    # Deterministic hue from the game name
    $hash = 17
    foreach ($ch in $GameName.ToCharArray()) {
        $hash = (($hash * 31) + [int]$ch) -band 0x7FFFFFFF
    }
    $hue = [double]($hash % 360)

    $colTop    = Convert-HslToRgbColor -H $hue -S 0.55 -L 0.22
    $colBottom = Convert-HslToRgbColor -H (($hue + 45) % 360) -S 0.50 -L 0.12
    $colAccent = Convert-HslToRgbColor -H $hue -S 0.70 -L 0.55

    $bmp = New-Object System.Drawing.Bitmap($Size, $Size, [System.Drawing.Imaging.PixelFormat]::Format32bppArgb)
    $g = [System.Drawing.Graphics]::FromImage($bmp)
    $font = $null
    try {
        $g.SmoothingMode = [System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode]::AntiAlias
        $g.TextRenderingHint = [System.Drawing.Text.TextRenderingHint]::AntiAlias

        # Diagonal gradient background
        $rect = New-Object System.Drawing.Rectangle(0, 0, $Size, $Size)
        $brush = New-Object System.Drawing.Drawing2D.LinearGradientBrush(
            $rect, $colTop, $colBottom,
            [System.Drawing.Drawing2D.LinearGradientMode]::ForwardDiagonal)
        $g.FillRectangle($brush, $rect)
        $brush.Dispose()

        # Subtle oversized watermark letter
        $initial = ($GameName.Trim())[0].ToString().ToUpperInvariant()
        $wmFont = New-Object System.Drawing.Font('Segoe UI', [float]($Size * 0.85), [System.Drawing.FontStyle]::Bold, [System.Drawing.GraphicsUnit]::Pixel)
        $wmBrush = New-Object System.Drawing.SolidBrush([System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(22, 255, 255, 255))
        $wmFormat = New-Object System.Drawing.StringFormat
        $wmFormat.Alignment = [System.Drawing.StringAlignment]::Center
        $wmFormat.LineAlignment = [System.Drawing.StringAlignment]::Center
        $g.DrawString($initial, $wmFont, $wmBrush, (New-Object System.Drawing.RectangleF(0, 0, $Size, $Size)), $wmFormat)
        $wmFont.Dispose(); $wmBrush.Dispose(); $wmFormat.Dispose()

        # Accent bar
        $accBrush = New-Object System.Drawing.SolidBrush($colAccent)
        $g.FillRectangle($accBrush, [int]($Size * 0.42), [int]($Size * 0.30), [int]($Size * 0.16), 6)
        $accBrush.Dispose()

        # Title text: shrink font until it fits
        $pad = [int]($Size * 0.10)
        $layout = New-Object System.Drawing.RectangleF($pad, [int]($Size * 0.36), ($Size - 2 * $pad), [int]($Size * 0.42))
        $format = New-Object System.Drawing.StringFormat
        $format.Alignment = [System.Drawing.StringAlignment]::Center
        $format.LineAlignment = [System.Drawing.StringAlignment]::Center

        $fontSize = [int]($Size * 0.14)
        while ($fontSize -gt 18) {
            if ($font) { $font.Dispose() }
            $font = New-Object System.Drawing.Font('Segoe UI', [float]$fontSize, [System.Drawing.FontStyle]::Bold, [System.Drawing.GraphicsUnit]::Pixel)
            $measured = $g.MeasureString($GameName, $font, [int]$layout.Width, $format)
            if ($measured.Height -le $layout.Height) { break }
            $fontSize -= 4
        }

        # Text shadow + text
        $shadowBrush = New-Object System.Drawing.SolidBrush([System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(140, 0, 0, 0))
        $shadowLayout = New-Object System.Drawing.RectangleF(($layout.X + 2), ($layout.Y + 3), $layout.Width, $layout.Height)
        $g.DrawString($GameName, $font, $shadowBrush, $shadowLayout, $format)
        $textBrush = New-Object System.Drawing.SolidBrush([System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(245, 255, 255, 255))
        $g.DrawString($GameName, $font, $textBrush, $layout, $format)
        $shadowBrush.Dispose(); $textBrush.Dispose(); $format.Dispose()

        $bmp.Save($OutputPath, [System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png)
    } catch {
        throw "Placeholder cover generation failed for '$GameName': $($_.Exception.Message)"
    } finally {
        if ($font) { $font.Dispose() }
        $g.Dispose()
        $bmp.Dispose()
    }
    return $OutputPath
}
```

```ps1
# ============================================================
#  Logger.ps1 - File + console logging with timestamps
# ============================================================

$script:LogFile = $null

function Initialize-Logger {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Root)

    $logDir = Join-Path $Root 'Logs'
    if (-not (Test-Path $logDir)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $logDir -Force | Out-Null
    }
    $script:LogFile = Join-Path $logDir 'Updater.log'

    Write-Log "============================================================" 'INFO' $true
    Write-Log "SteamGridDB Icon Updater Ultimate - session started" 'INFO' $true
    Write-Log "PowerShell $($PSVersionTable.PSVersion) on $([System.Environment]::OSVersion.VersionString)" 'INFO' $true
}

function Write-Log {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Message,
        [ValidateSet('INFO','OK','WARN','ERROR','DEBUG')][string]$Level = 'INFO',
        [bool]$FileOnly = $false
    )

    $timestamp = Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'
    $line = "[$timestamp] [$Level] $Message"

    if ($script:LogFile) {
        try {
            Add-Content -Path $script:LogFile -Value $line -Encoding UTF8 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        } catch { }
    }

    if (-not $FileOnly) {
        switch ($Level) {
            'OK'    { Write-Host $line -ForegroundColor Green }
            'WARN'  { Write-Host $line -ForegroundColor Yellow }
            'ERROR' { Write-Host $line -ForegroundColor Red }
            'DEBUG' { Write-Host $line -ForegroundColor DarkGray }
            default { Write-Host $line -ForegroundColor Gray }
        }
    }
}
```
